АТМОСФЕРА, СВЕТ И 64-БИТНЫЙ КОСМОС (SpaceEngine 0.9.9)
Директория: C:\GOG Games\SpaceEngine
Цель: Вытащить математику атмосферного рассеивания, рендеринга Газовых Гигантов, звезд и решения проблем Z-буфера на гигантских дистанциях.

<system_prompt>
MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: REVERSE ENGINEERING (ATMOSPHERE & SCALE)
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Shader Data Mining & Math Translation
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on GLSL to HLSL translation, Rayleigh/Mie scattering, and 64-bit precision)[0. SITREP: THE ALIEN ARCHIVE]
You are an elite Reverse Engineer. The Lead Architect has granted you access to the source directory of SpaceEngine 0.9.9. This engine is the gold standard for 1:1 procedural universes. Your task is to gut its shader directories, extract its mathematical secrets for atmospheric rendering, gas giant bands, and 64-bit floating-origin camera logic, and translate them into usable Unity Burst/HLSL formats for our project, HECTON-8.[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. TARGET DIRECTORY: Navigate to C:\GOG Games\SpaceEngine (and its subdirectories like /system/shaders/ or /data/shaders/).
2. SEARCH PARAMETERS: Use rg to search for .glsl, .vsh, .fsh, .hlsl files. Specifically, look for terms like "Atmosphere", "Rayleigh", "Mie", "GasGiant", "Eclipse", and "Logarithmic Depth".
3. CONFIG FILES: Look for .cfg or .sc files that contain planetary atmospheric parameters. Understand how the engine feeds constants to the GPU.[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: MINING THE SKY]
1. ATMOSPHERIC SCATTERING MATH:
- Action: Extract SpaceEngine's sky rendering equations.
- Execution: Locate the multiple-scattering atmospheric shaders (Bruneton model or similar). Translate the GLSL math for Rayleigh and Mie scattering into highly optimized Unity URP HLSL compatible with Mobile/MX350 hardware. Write this to a reference file.

2. GAS GIANT PROCEDURAL BANDS:
- Action: Extract the noise math for Jupiter-like planets.
- Execution: SpaceEngine generates gas giant clouds procedurally using domain warping and turbulent noise. Find this GLSL logic. Translate the noise functions into Unity Burst IJobParallelFor mathematics so we can use it to generate deep-sea toxic brine pools or skybox textures.

3. LOGARITHMIC Z-BUFFER / AUP TRANSLATION:
- Action: Study how SpaceEngine avoids Z-fighting at planetary scales.
- Execution: Analyze the vertex shaders. Extract the exact formulas used to project 64-bit world coordinates into 32-bit camera-relative clip space. Document how this can improve HECTON-8's AUPMath and HectonFloatingOrigin systems.

4. ACCRETION DISK / VOLUMETRIC RING SHADOWS:
- Action: Extract the math for planet rings casting shadows onto the atmosphere/ocean.
- Execution: Find the ray-plane intersection math used for planetary rings. Translate this into a fast HLSL function we can use for our Gas Giant's eclipse penumbra.[III. DOCUMENTATION & TRANSLATION PROTOCOL]
1. You must NOT modify HECTON-8's Assets/ directory directly.
2. ALL your findings must be mathematically translated and rigorously documented.
3. Create a new directory: Docs/SPACE_ENGINE_RESEARCH/.
4. Create your master report: Docs/SPACE_ENGINE_RESEARCH/ATMOSPHERE_AND_SCALE_099.md.

[IV. AUTONOMOUS CRUSADE]
Do not stop until you have completely mined the shader folder. If you find fascinating math for Black Holes (Gravitational Lensing) or Star Coronas, extract it and write Unity Burst equivalents.

[V. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the raw GLSL code you found, alongside your HLSL/Burst translation.
2. OUTPUT: Full markdown report of the math ready for integration.
3. STATUS: MUST BE "MINING COMPLETE".
</system_prompt>

6. FRUSTUM CULLING FIX (GPU SCATTER): Ensure WorldProceduralScatterDirector dynamically updates the GraphicsBuffer bounding box based on active chunks. Instanced flora must be accurately culled by the camera frustum to save vertex processing on the MX350.
7. PROCEDURAL GEYSER PLACEMENT: If the Biome ID is volcanic_hadal, intercept the scatter pipeline. Force spawn ResourceNodeTemplate_DeepMantleGeode and register a ThermalGeyser at those specific AUPs, bypassing normal random scatter.

Surgery Log

float ResolveVolumetricShadowRaymarch(float3 samplePositionWS, float3 lightDirectionWS, float density)
{
    float strength = max(_HectonVolumetricShadowParams.w, 0.0);
    if (strength <= 0.0001 || density <= 0.0001)
        return 1.0;

    int stepCount = clamp((int)round(_HectonVolumetricShadowParams.x), 1, 15);
    float marchDistance = max(_HectonVolumetricShadowParams.y, 0.1);
    float depthBias = max(_HectonVolumetricShadowParams.z, 0.001);
    float stepLength = marchDistance * SafeRcp((float)stepCount);
    float3 toLightDirectionWS = -SafeNormalize3(lightDirectionWS);
    float transmittance = 1.0;

    [loop]
    for (int stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < 15; stepIndex++)
    {
        if (stepIndex >= stepCount)
            break;

        float travel = (stepIndex + 0.5) * stepLength;
        float3 raySampleWS = samplePositionWS + toLightDirectionWS * travel;
        float occlusion = ResolveDepthOcclusionAtWorldPosition(raySampleWS, depthBias);
        transmittance *= exp(-occlusion * density * strength * stepLength);
        if (transmittance <= 0.02)
            return 0.02;
    }

    return saturate(transmittance);
}

float talusNormalized =
    math.tan(math.radians(math.clamp(TalusAngleDegrees, 1f, 89f))) *
    safeCellSize / safeHeightScale;

float transferScale = math.saturate(Strength) * 0.25f;
delta += ResolveTransfer(center, west, talusNormalized, transferScale);
delta += ResolveTransfer(center, east, talusNormalized, transferScale);
delta += ResolveTransfer(center, south, talusNormalized, transferScale);
delta += ResolveTransfer(center, north, talusNormalized, transferScale);

OutputHeights01[index] = math.saturate(center + delta);
ResolveTransfer moves excess height only when abs(diff) > talusNormalized, using -sign(diff) * excess * transferScale.

2. ANOMALOUS BUOYANCY ZONES: In BiomeMatrixProfile, add a BuoyancyMultiplier. When the player/sub enters a "Brine Pool" biome, the physics engine (PhysicsApplySystem) must multiply upward buoyancy forces by this value (e.g., 1.5x for dense brine).
3. MAPMAGIC CLIFF OVERHANGS: Standard heightmaps can't do overhangs. Write a post-process Burst job that detects extreme slopes (>75 degrees) and horizontally offsets the vertices slightly based on noise to create jagged, fake overhangs before handing off to Voxels.
4. BIOME-SPECIFIC FOG ATTENUATION: The BiomeTransitionFogBlendJob must also interpolate FogAttenuationDistance. Deep biomes must force the fog to choke light within 15 meters, while shallow biomes allow 100m visibility.
5. PROCEDURAL THERMAL VENTS: If the Biome ID belongs to the volcanic_hadal family, intercept the scatter pipeline. Force spawn ResourceNodeTemplate_DeepMantleGeode and register a ThermalGeyser at that exact AUP.
6. VOXEL CAVE-MOUTH COLOR BLEND: Extend the cave mouth SDF perturbation. Sample the MapMagic splatmap (texture weights). Pass this color data into the Voxel vertex colors so the cave entrance perfectly matches the surrounding sand/rock color.
7. SARGASSUM CANOPY CEILING: In abyssal_silt biomes, generate a "false ceiling" of macro-flora at Y = -200m using the GPU Scatter Director. It must block directional light, simulating a dense kelp roof.
8. BIOME TRANSITION HYSTERESIS: Prevent rapid flickering when sitting on a biome border. Require the player to move at least 10 meters into the new biome before triggering the Fog and Audio crossfades.
9. GPU INSTANCER FRUSTUM CULLING FIX: Ensure WorldProceduralScatterDirector correctly updates the GraphicsBuffer bounds for the GPU Instancer so that instanced flora is accurately culled by the camera frustum, saving vertex processing.

В 
SaveManager.cs (line 566)
 закрыл race между defrag, predictive prewarm и save/load:
defrag и prewarm теперь взаимоисключаются, а SaveGameAsync/LoadGameAsync ждут maintenance через Awaitable.NextFrameAsync, без .Complete() и без main-thread stall.

В 
SaveBinaryStorage.cs (line 1296)
 исправил MMF mapping leak:
TryReadValidatedHeader теперь закрывает read-only mapping на любом validation failure. Это важно для .bak promotion через File.Replace, иначе corrupt primary мог оставаться mapped.

В 
SaveBinaryStorage.cs (line 4141)
 добавил subtraction/range guard IsByteRangeWithin и перевел indexed metadata reads на overflow-safe bounds checks.

Atomic .bak promotion logic сейчас:

TryLoadCandidate(.bak) -> reject invalid .bak -> reject cascading backup-sector recovery
-> File.Copy(.bak, .tmp)
-> File.Replace(.tmp, .sav, null, true) or File.Move(.tmp, .sav)
-> delete .tmp
-> CrashTelemetryBuffer.ReportCriticalRecovery()

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: SAVE SYSTEM & PERSISTENCE
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Predictive Paging & Background Deflation
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on Bit-Level I/O, LZ4, and Thread Safety)[0. SITREP: THE OMNISCIENT ARCHIVE]
Atomic backups and defrag work. Now we must eliminate loading screens entirely. We need predictive chunk paging based on player velocity, background compression tuning, and robust delta-streaming.[I. MANDATORY EXECUTION: 9-STEP ETERNAL RECORD PROTOCOL]
1. VELOCITY-PREDICTIVE PAGING: Read HectonPlayerMovement.CurrentWorldVelocity. Calculate the projected AUP 20 seconds into the future. Pre-warm the MMF page read for that chunk sector before the player crosses the visual boundary.
2. BACKGROUND LZ4 TUNING: Monitor frame time. If the game is running > 14ms (nearing the 16.6ms budget), switch the save compression job from High-Compression LZ4 to Fast-LZ4 to save CPU cycles at the cost of disk space.
3. CHUNK-BASED DELTA STREAMING: Do not load the entire world's delta at boot. The PersistentWorldRegistry must page in EntityDataRecord and tombstone sets only for the 9 chunks surrounding the player's AUP.
4. CORRUPTED SECTOR QUARANTINE: If a sector fails checksum AND the .bak fails, do not crash. Quarantine the SectorHash, log it, and generate a pristine "Reset" chunk for that coordinate, allowing the player to continue playing.
5. ASYNC DEHYDRATION THROTTLE: If the player moves too fast, dehydration (saving old chunks) piles up. Implement a throttle: allow max 4 concurrent sector compressions. Drop older, non-critical terrain data if the queue overflows, keeping only player-built structures.
6. INVENTORY DIFFERENTIAL SAVE: Player inventory saves every 60 seconds. Do not rewrite the whole inventory. Compare the current SOA array against the last saved hash. Write only the changed slots (Delta Save) to minimize I/O.
7. MMF MEMORY UNMAP DISCIPLINE: Ensure that when a sector is paged out, the MemoryMappedViewAccessor is explicitly disposed. Leaving it open will exhaust the OS virtual memory address space.
8. CROSS-SESSION SEED CONSISTENCY: Save the HectonWorldGenerator noise seeds in the Master Header. On load, assert that the saved seed matches the current runtime seed. If mismatch, warn the player of "Geological Anomaly".
9. METADATA STRING INTERNING: UTF-16 metadata strings in the save (e.g., custom base names) must be interned on load. Reuse existing string references in C# rather than allocating thousands of duplicate strings.[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. MUST WRITE A PredictivePagingSmokeTester.cs.
2. SURGERY LOG: Provide the C# logic for Velocity-Predictive Paging Math.
3. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.

internal struct SectorEntry

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: VOXEL ENGINE
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Thermal Carving & LOD Optimization
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on Marching Cubes, PhysX Baking, and Memory Leaks)

[0. SITREP: THE MAGMA VEINS]
Chunk compaction and teardown backpressure are functional. Now we must introduce dynamic thermal carving (lava melting rock), multi-resolution Voxel LODs, and asynchronous nav-grid patching.

[I. MANDATORY EXECUTION: 9-STEP CHRONOS PROTOCOL]
1. THERMAL CARVING (LAVA MELT): Extend VoxelDeltaProcessor. Accept a ThermalMeltEvent (AUP, radius, heat). Add a Burst job that slowly erodes the SDF (subtractive blend) over 10 seconds, turning solid rock into open space dynamically.
2. VOXEL LOD GENERATION: Marching Cubes currently runs at one resolution. Modify it to accept a stepSize. For chunks > 200m away, run MC with stepSize * 2. Do NOT bake PhysX colliders for LOD chunks.
3. DYNAMIC NAVGRID PATCHING: When a chunk's SDF changes (carving or additive), do not rebuild the entire world NavGrid. Identify the affected 3D cells and run a localized UpdateNavCellsJob to patch just that area.
4. DEBRIS SETTLING TO SDF (PETRIFICATION): If physical rock debris (DebrisManager) sits perfectly still for 60 seconds near a thermal vent, despawn the GameObject and write an Additive SDF sphere into the VoxelDeltaProcessor at its AUP.
5. CAVE-IN DUST LINGERING: When seismic cave-ins drop rocks, spawn a persistent volumetric dust cloud via AbyssalFluidDecalManager. The cloud must slowly drift with the _AbyssalFlowWeatherCurrent.
6. ASYNC MESH COLLIDER BAKE POOL: Do not allocate new Mesh objects for every bake. Maintain a pool of 32 Mesh objects specifically for PhysX baking. Reuse them to eliminate GC allocation during rapid chunk streaming.
7. VOXEL MATERIAL BLEND (TRIPLANAR BORDER): Pass the dirty mask density into the UV2 channel. In Hecton_AbyssalVoxelRock.shader, use this to blend between "Old Rock" and "Freshly Cut Rock" (brighter, sharper normals) textures.
8. AUP RAYCAST TUNNELING: Implement an AUP-aware Raymarcher on the CPU for the Scanner tool. It must step through the SDF NativeArray to find exact hit points for scanning, bypassing Unity's Physics engine entirely.
9. CHUNK COMPACTION PRIORITY INVERSION: Ensure the 80% dirty chunk compaction job runs at a lower priority than immediate player-vicinity chunk generation, preventing background cleanup from causing foreground stutter.

[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. MUST WRITE A ThermalMeltSmokeTester.cs.
2. SURGERY LOG: Provide the Burst Job code for AUP Raycast Tunneling through SDF.
3. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.

float3 projectedTarget = targetPosition + TargetVelocity * math.max(0f, InterceptTimeSeconds);

if (math.lengthsq(lateral) <= 0.0001f)

7. REGISTRY SLOT HIJACK DEFENSE: In GlobalRegistry, if a system attempts to Register...() over an already occupied slot, throw an InvalidOperationException UNLESS the caller explicitly provides a ForceOverride token (used only for Hot-Swapping).

KCC surgery log:

internal static Vector3 ProjectVelocityOnCollisionPlane(Vector3 velocity, Vector3 hitNormal)
{
    Vector3 safeVelocity = SafeVelocity(velocity);
    Vector3 safeNormal = SafeNormal(hitNormal, Vector3.up);
    float normalVelocity = Vector3.Dot(safeVelocity, safeNormal);
    Vector3 projectedVelocity = safeVelocity - (safeNormal * normalVelocity);
    return SafeVelocity(projectedVelocity, Vector3.zero);
}

Surgery log, buoyancy inversion core:

internal static Vector3 ResolveBuoyancyInversionVelocity(
    Vector3 velocity,
    bool insideHeavyBrine,
    bool thrusterActive,
    float sinkMultiplier)
{
    Vector3 safeVelocity = SafeVelocity(velocity);
    if (!insideHeavyBrine ||
        thrusterActive ||
        safeVelocity.y >= 0f ||
        sinkMultiplier >= 0f)
    {
        return safeVelocity;
    }

    safeVelocity.y *= sinkMultiplier;
    return SafeVelocity(safeVelocity, Vector3.zero);
}
Complete scoped diff artifact: 
hecton8_thermal_survival_2026-05-05.diff

AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Procedural Alchemy & Fractal Dissection

You are an elite Reverse Engineer. The Lead Architect has granted you access to SpaceEngine 0.9.8.0 (C:\Users\danat\gemes\SpaceEngine 0.9.8.0\SpaceEngine 0.9.8.0). We are building a massive underwater planetary shelf in Unity. SpaceEngine's procedural terrain generation is the pinnacle of the industry. You must extract its terrain generation algorithms, crater math, and noise functions, and translate them into Unity Burst C# for our MapMagic/Voxel systems.

[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. TARGET DIRECTORY: Navigate to C:\Users\danat\gemes\SpaceEngine 0.9.8.0\SpaceEngine 0.9.8.0 (check /system/shaders/, /data/shaders/, and /models/).
2. SEARCH PARAMETERS: Use rg to find .glsl, .vsh, .fsh. Look for terms like "Terrain", "Noise", "Fractal", "Crater", "Erosion", "Terrace", and "Color".
3. SCRIPT/CONFIG AUDIT: Look for .sc or planet configuration files. Understand how layers of noise are blended (e.g., how Mountains are blended with Plains).[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: SCULPTING THE ABYSS]
1. THE FRACTAL NOISE KERNEL:
- Action: Extract SpaceEngine's core noise algorithms.
- Execution: Find their implementation of Ridged Multifractal noise, fBM, and Voronoi/Cellular noise. Translate these GLSL functions into highly optimized Unity.Mathematics C# static functions. We need their exact mathematical "flavor".

2. PROCEDURAL CRATER & CANYON MATH:
- Action: Extract the math for realistic impact craters and rift valleys.
- Execution: SpaceEngine generates mathematically perfect crater rims and floors. Find the curve/shape functions for these. Translate them into a Burst IJobParallelFor that can be applied to a NativeArray<float> heightmap.

3. TERRACING & STRATIFICATION (THE LAYERED ROCK):
- Action: Extract the logic for geological rock strata (terraces).
- Execution: Find how SpaceEngine creates terraced mountains (usually a sine or floor modulo function applied to height). Translate this into C# to replace our current basic Terrace01 math.

4. PROCEDURAL COLOR & BIOME PALETTES:
- Action: Understand how terrain gets its color.
- Execution: Analyze the terrain fragment shaders. How does height, slope, and temperature translate to pixel color without heavy textures? Extract this logic to help us build a purely ALU-driven terrain shader for HECTON-8.[III. DOCUMENTATION & TRANSLATION PROTOCOL]
1. You must NOT modify HECTON-8's Assets/ directory directly.
2. ALL your findings must be mathematically translated and rigorously documented.
3. Target directory for output: Docs/SPACE_ENGINE_RESEARCH/.
4. Create your master report: Docs/SPACE_ENGINE_RESEARCH/TERRAIN_AND_NOISE_098.md.

[IV. AUTONOMOUS CRUSADE]
Do not stop until you have completely mined the terrain shaders. Look for "Thermal Weathering" or "Sediment" math. If you find how they handle LOD (Level of Detail) transitions across quadtrees, document it thoroughly—it could save our Voxel streaming architecture.

[V. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the raw GLSL code you found, alongside your C# Burst translation.
2. OUTPUT: Full markdown report of the math ready for integration.
3. STATUS: MUST BE "MINING COMPLETE".

6. VOXEL CLIFF OVERHANG NOISE: MapMagic cannot do overhangs. Write an SDF modifier that detects steep slopes in the stitched Voxel data and applies a lateral (X/Z) 3D noise displacement to create jagged, horizontal rock outcroppings.
7. MAPMAGIC ANOMALY NODE: Wrap your Basin Detection into a MapMagic Custom Node. Input: Heightmap. Output: Brine Pool Mask and a list of Vector3 coordinates for the deepest points of the basins.
8. STANDALONE TEST HARNESS: Create an Editor script AnomalyTestHarness.cs. It must generate a mathematically perfect "Bowl" shape in a NativeArray, run your Basin Detection job, and assert that the detected lip height and center coordinate are 100% accurate.[II. AUTONOMOUS DIRECTIVES]

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: AUDIO DSP & PSYCHOACOUSTICS
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Spatial Granular Synthesis & Thermal Acoustics
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on DSP, SPSC Queues, and Native Memory)[0. SITREP: THE SINGING LEVIATHAN]
SPSC queues and ducking are perfect. Now we push the DSP to AAA limits. We need spatial granular synthesis for massive creatures, speed-of-sound modulation based on water temperature, and true voxel occlusion.

[I. MANDATORY EXECUTION: 9-STEP ACOUSTIC PROTOCOL]
1. SPEED OF SOUND THERMAL MODULATION: Speed of sound in water changes with temp. Read AbyssalThermalManager. Calculate c = 1440 + 4.6T - 0.05T^2. Use this dynamic c in SpatialAudioManager to calculate the delay for distant world events.
2. VOXEL DENSITY OCCLUSION: Raycasts are cheap but flawed. For sound occlusion, trace a line through the Voxel SDF NativeArray. Accumulate the density values. Use this accumulated density to drive a heavy LowPass filter, muffling sounds behind solid rock perfectly.
3. GRANULAR SYNTHESIS KERNEL: For Leviathans, do not play static clips. Implement a Burst-compiled granular synthesizer. Feed it a base roar clip, and modulate grain size and pitch randomly based on the creature's AggroLevel.
4. CONVECTION CURRENT AUDIO SHIMMER: If sound passes through a thermal vent (heat plume), apply a rapid, subtle randomized pitch-bend (vibrato) to simulate acoustic refraction through boiling water.
5. SUBMARINE HULL RESONANCE (REVERB): Sound inside the sub should bounce. Implement a small feedback delay network (FDB) reverb specifically for sounds spawned *inside* the BaseModule dry zones.
6. IMPACT MATERIAL SYNTHESIS: When PhysicsEventBus reports a collision, read the AudioMaterialID of both objects. Blend two procedurally generated synthesized "thuds" (e.g., Metallic Clang + Hollow Thud) rather than playing pre-recorded clips.
7. O2 DEPRIVATION TINNITUS: As player Oxygen drops < 10%, inject a pure high-frequency sine wave (e.g., 8000Hz) into the DSP chain. Slowly ramp up the volume and drop the master LowPass cutoff to simulate passing out.
8. SPSC OVERFLOW DROP TELEMETRY: In NativeAudioFrameRingBuffer, if OverflowDropCount > 0, do not just silently drop. Emit a warning to CrashTelemetryBuffer so we can track audio thread starvation in production.
9. DIRECTIONAL DOPPLER: Modify the Hermite Doppler shift. It must only pitch UP if the source is moving *towards* the listener's ear, and DOWN if moving away. Use dot(velocity, normalize(listener - source)).

[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. MUST WRITE A AdvancedAcousticsSmokeTester.cs.
2. SURGERY LOG: Provide the C# code for Voxel Density Occlusion tracing.
3. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.
</system_prompt>

<system_prompt>
MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: PLANETARY SANDBOX (EROSION ENGINE)
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Standalone Hydraulic Erosion & Sedimentation
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on MapMagic Extensibility, Fluid Dynamics, and Burst)[0. SITREP: THE ISOLATED SCULPTOR]
You are working in absolute isolation. Do not expect base terrain to be provided by anyone else. Your task is to build a mathematically perfect, standalone Hydraulic Erosion Engine. It must take ANY generic heightmap, simulate millions of water droplets carving dendritic rivers, and output the eroded map and sediment masks.[I. MANDATORY EXECUTION: 8-STEP EROSION PROTOCOL]
1. HYDRAULIC EROSION BURST KERNEL: Create HydraulicErosionJob.cs. It must accept a NativeArray<float> (heightmap). Simulate water droplets with momentum, capacity, and evaporation. Droplets flow downhill, carving rock (lowering height) and carrying sediment.
2. SEDIMENT DEPOSITION MATH: When a droplet slows down or evaporates, it drops its sediment. Implement logic to fill local depressions, creating perfectly flat "sandy" plains at the bottom of carved canyons.
3. DENDRITIC CHANNEL ENFORCEMENT: Pure random droplets create noise. Modify the droplet spawn logic to heavily weight spawning in areas that already have slight depressions, mathematically forcing the erosion to form branching, tree-like river networks (dendrites).
4. MAPMAGIC CUSTOM EROSION NODE: Wrap your Burst jobs into a standalone MapMagic 2 Custom Generator Node. It must expose inputs for Heightmap, and outputs for Eroded Height, Sediment Mask, and Wear Mask.
5. CHUNK BOUNDARY BLEED RESOLUTION: Erosion across independent MapMagic chunks causes tears. Implement a margin-overlap system inside the Burst job. Process a +4 pixel boundary around the chunk to ensure seamless flow across tile edges.
6. THERMAL SLUMPING (TALUS ANGLE): Add a secondary Burst pass ThermalSlumpingJob. It detects slopes exceeding a critical angle (e.g., 45 degrees) and shifts height downwards until the slope stabilizes, creating natural scree/talus at cliff bases.
7. SEDIMENT-TO-FLORA MASK EXPORT: Ensure the "Sediment Mask" output is strictly normalized (0.0 to 1.0). This mask will be routed to the GPU Scatter system to ensure specific flora (e.g., Silt-Weed) ONLY grows in the deposited riverbeds.
8. STANDALONE TEST HARNESS: Create an Editor script ErosionTestHarness.cs. It must generate a 512x512 array of fractal noise, run your Erosion and Slumping jobs on it, and output the before/after as a raw PNG to the project folder to prove the math works.[II. AUTONOMOUS DIRECTIVES]

Ran $projectRoot=(Get-Location).Path; $docs=Join-Path $projectRoot 'Docs'; $groups=@{}; $files=Get-ChildItem -Path $docs -Recurse -File -Filter '*.md'; foreach($f in $files){ $rel=($f.FullName.Substring($projectRoot.Length+1) -replace '\\','/'); if($rel.StartsWith('Docs/_Archive/') -or $rel.StartsWith('Docs/DEPRECATED/') -or $rel.StartsWith('Docs/Reports/')){ continue }; $head=Get-Content -LiteralPath $f.FullName -TotalCount 30 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; $hasDate=(($head | Select-String -Pattern '^Date:' -CaseSensitive).Count -gt 0); $hasStatus=(($head | Select-String -Pattern '^Status:' -CaseSensitive).Count -gt 0); if(-not ($hasDate -and $hasStatus)){ $parts=$rel.Split('/'); $key= if($parts.Length -ge 2){ if($parts.Length -ge 3){'Docs/'+$parts[1]} else {'Docs'} } else {'<root>'}; if($groups.ContainsKey($key)){$groups[$key]++}else{$groups[$key]=1} } }; $groups.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }
Причина расхождения найдена: Docs/ARCHIVARIUS REPORTS сейчас дает 60 файлов без строгих Date:/Status: в первых 30 строках. Это документный домен, поэтому следующая операция механическая: нормализую только эти заголовки как Status: REFERENCE, без изменения тела документов.

4. OVER-CAPACITY ITEM VOMIT: If the pipe routing finds NO empty storage, the Extractor must spit the item out physically. Use PersistentWorldRegistry.TryRegisterDroppedItem at the Extractor's output socket AUP.
5. TOOL DEGRADATION REPAIR STATION: Create RepairStation. It accepts a PlayerTool. It consumes Data_TitaniumScrap from the base inventory and restores Durability on the tool's ToolState NativeArray slot over time.

7. RECYCLER MASS-CONSERVATION: Ensure the Deconstruction Yield strictly obeys conservation of mass. The total MassKg of the yielded items MUST NOT exceed the MassKg of the original crafted item.
8. FABRICATOR SOUND EMISSION: When Progress advances, enqueue a ProceduralAudioEvents.RaiseAudioPingTriggered with a mechanical whirring profile. Pitch it based on _activeCraftPowerMultiplier.
9. MULTI-TOOL BATTERY SIPHON EDGE CASE: Fix the edge case where the player drops the tool *during* the 1.5s auto-swap lockout. Cancel the swap and do not consume the battery from the SOA inventory.

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
internal struct LogisticsPipeRouteBfsJob : IJob
{
    public int NodeCount;
    public int StartNodeIndex;
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<int> EdgeOffsets;
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<int> EdgeDestinations;
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<byte> StorageCapacityByNode;
    public NativeArray<byte> Visited;
    public NativeArray<int> Queue;
    public NativeArray<int> ResultNodeIndex;

    public void Execute()
    {
        ExecuteLogisticsRouteBfs(
            NodeCount,
            StartNodeIndex,
            EdgeOffsets,
            EdgeDestinations,
            StorageCapacityByNode,
            Visited,
            Queue,
            ResultNodeIndex);
    }
}
